The LP: Given f (nonnegative, normalized maybe S=√(2n)?), M=||f*f||∞. Maximize ∑ b_j subject to b_j≥0 and (f*b)_k ≤ M for k=0..2n-2. Note f*b is convolution (linear in b). This LP provides a direction g with sum large and whose convolution with f is bounded. Then rescale g to normalization and mix f with g. This is a kind of Bregman/convex optimization. So P mass at high degree is lost in A_n, but its square contributes to C_{n-1}? For C_{n-1}, pairs a_i a_{n-1-i}; a_i includes P up to i, a_{n-1-i} up to n-1-i. The sum of P pair weights at k=n-1 is ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} (∑_{p≤i} c_{i-p}P_p)(∑_{q≤n-1-i} c_{n-1-i-q}P_q). Not simply prefix P^2 at n-1 because c weights. For base P=1, center=1. For P with mass at high p, it can contribute to center through combinations p and n-1-p? If P mass near n, p≤i and q≤n-1-i cannot both be high unless sum p+q≤n-1; so high P beyond n-1 not all used. P1^2 not necessarily reached. My earlier infinite truncation argument flawed for A=C_n P truncated. We can exploit high-degree P to increase sum? But if P mass high doesn't contribute to A sum, not useful.

This suggests general A(z) = C_n P truncated is flexible; the shape of A might be optimized via P. But that's equivalent to any A (since C_n has positive coefficients, solving P=A*C_n^{-1} may have negative coefficients). Not simpler.

Maybe we can use frequency domain to design A with nonnegative coefficients via phase retrieval. But too much.

Helper _est_score(a):
```
def _score_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size ==0: return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <0.01: return np.inf
    n=a.size
    C=_conv_full(a)
    M=float(np.max(C))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*M/(S*S))
```
Conv_full uses a already nonnegative finite.

_conv_full:
```
def _conv_full(a):
    n=a.size
    m=a.size + a.size -1
    size=1
    while size<m: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)[:m]
    # remove tiny negative
    if c.size:
       c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
Use fa*fa(element-wise) not fa*fa? complex squared okay. For real convolution, fa*fa (not abs squared) because linear convolution of same signal: FFT(a)^2 (not |FFT|^2). Since a real not symmetric, product of complex with itself gives correct complex (imag should zero). Use fa*fa.

_conv_valid_w_a(W,a): W len L=2n-1, a len n.
```
size=1; total=len(W)+len(a)-1; while size<total: size<<=1
fw=np.fft.rfft(W,size); fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],size)
full=np.fft.irfft(fw*fa,size)
return full[n-1:3*n-2] # length n? total? Start n-1, length n
```
Check indices: full length total =3n-2. Valid j=0..n-1 at k=n-1+j => n-1 ..2n-2 inclusive length n. Good.

Gradient:
```
def _gradient_smooth(a,p):
   n=a.size; S=np.sum(a); C=_conv_full(a)
   Cp = C**p  # for p=4
   sumCp = np.sum(Cp)
   W = C**(p-1)
   D = _conv_valid(W,a)
   Spow = S**(2*p)
   Spow1 = Spow*S
   grad = 2.0*p*n*D/Spow - 4.0*p*n*sumCp/Spow1
   return grad*C? no grad. Also exact score.
```
For p=4, D len n. If S very large? a bounded 1000, n7000 S~7e6 S^8 huge 5e49? Python float up to1e308, okay? 7e6^8=5.7e51. D maybe huge? Grad underflow? We keep z bounds and scale mean0, S around base 100, not 7e6 because c scale. Good.

```
def _adam_full(base, start_z, deadline, p=4, lr=0.02):
   z=start_z.copy(); m=np.zeros_like; v=...
   a=base*np.exp(z); best_z=z.copy(); best_score=_score_fast(a)
   t=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       t+=1
       a=base*np.exp(z); a=np.clip(a,0,1000) # if clip, gradient mismatch; but z bound enough. Could still clip no grad.
       grad_a,_? = _gradient_smooth(a,p)
       grad_z=grad_a*a
       grad_z -= np.mean(grad_z)
       # optional scale grad by 1/(0.01+np.std(a)? no)
       m=...; v=...
       update=m_hat/(np.sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8)
       # damp update
       step = lr*update
       # If step has large norm, scale
       norm=np.linalg.norm(step)
       if norm>0.5: step *=0.5/norm
       z_new=z+step
       z_new=np.clip(z_new,-20,5)
       z_new-=z_new.mean()
       a_new=base*np.exp(z_new)
       score_new=_score_fast(a_new)
       if score_new < best_score:
          best_z=z_new.copy(); best_score=score_new; z=z_new
       else:
          # accept with simulated annealing small prob? maybe if t%100 and score_new < best_score*1.001
          if score_new < best_score*1.002 and np.random.random()<0.01:
              z=z_new
       if t%1000==0: print progress
   return base*np.exp(best_z), best_score, best_z
```


Pair swap preserving sum:
```
def _pair_swap(a, rng):
   n=a.size
   # choose i weighted by a (early mass), j weighted uniform/later
   i = rng.choice(n, p=a/a.sum()) if sum>0 else rng.integers(n)
   side = rng.random()
   if side<0.5:
       # transfer to late
       j = rng.integers(max(0,a.size//2), a.size)
   else:
       j = rng.choice(n, p=a/a.sum())
   frac = float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
   t=a[i]*frac
   if t<=0 or t>=a[i]: return a
   cand=a.copy(); cand[i]-=t; cand[j]+=t
   return cand
```
If i==j no.
Could also block swap: transfer block mass from early to late.

```
cand[start:start+width]*=scale
# maybe normalize to keep sum? not necessary
```

Family details:
```
def _make_family(n, alpha, A):
    c = base_c  # precomputed
    if alpha==0:
       b = np.ones(n)
    else:
       idx = np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
       b = idx ** (-alpha)
    b_rev = b[::-1]
    scale = A * (n ** (alpha - 0.5))
    a = c + scale * b_rev
    return a
```
Alpha 0 exactly: scale A*n^-0.5 =A/√n; b one. This is constant addition.

Let's implement family search robustly:
```
def _family_score(base_c, n, params):
    alpha = float(params[0]); A=float(params[1]); s=float(params[2])
    if alpha <0: alpha=0
    if s <=0: s=1e-3
    if A <0: A=0
    if alpha >0:
       x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + s
       b = x ** (-alpha)
    else: b = np.ones(n)
    scale = A * (n ** (alpha - 0.5))
    a = base_c + scale * b[::-1]
    # ensure finite, clip
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    return _score_fast(a)
```

For α=0, n**( -0.5)=1/√n.
For α near0, b ~ ones; scale A/√n.

Now local search from family best. We can implement block multiplicative and pair swaps. Since family best may have shape c+tail; block perturbations on log a.

Detailed _local_search(a, deadline):
```
rng=default_rng(seed)
current=a.copy(); current_s=score(current)
best=a.copy(); best_s=current_s
T=0.02; temp_min=0.001
# adaptive sigma block
sigma=0.2
for it in range(int(1e6)):
  if time>deadline: break
  r=rng.random()
  if r<0.4:
     # block log perturbation
     width=int(rng.integers(max(1,n//500), max(2,n//20)))
     start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
     noise=rng.normal(0,sigma,width)
     cand=current.copy(); cand[start:start+width]*=np.exp(noise)
  elif r<0.7:
     # pair swap preserving sum
     cand=current.copy()
     S=current.sum()
     if S>0:
       p=current/S
       i=int(rng.choice(n,p=p))
       if rng.random()<0.6:
          j=int(rng.integers(n//3,n))
       else: j=int(rng.choice(n,p=p))
       if i!=j:
          frac=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.6))
          t=current[i]*frac
          if t>0 and t<current[i]:
             cand[i]-=t; cand[j]+=t
     if i==j: continue
  else:
     # global smooth noise
     noise=rng.normal(0,sigma,n)
     # low-pass? just
     cand=current*np.exp(0.5*noise)
  cand=np.clip(cand,0,1000)
  sc=score(cand)
  if not finite: continue
  if sc < current_s or (sc > current_s and rng.random()<np.exp((current_s-sc)/T)):
     current=cand; current_s=sc
     if sc<best_s-1e-12:
       best=cand.copy(); best_s=sc
       T=max(temp_min,T*0.98)
       sigma=max(0.02, sigma*0.99)
  else:
     T=max(temp_min,T*0.999)
  if it%2000==0: print
return best,best_s
```
Accepting worse with T relative to score (~1.2), T 0.02 allows exp(-1) for 0.02 worse. Good.
Pair swap with i chosen by p (early), j late; may improve balance.
Global noise may destroy; we can reduce probability (0.1) and sigma small.

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)^2).

Strategy:
- Start from the central-binomial family a[j] = binom(2j,j)/4^j, whose first half
  of the self-convolution is exactly flat.
- Search a small finite-dimensional family that adds a weak endpoint tail:
  a = base + A * n^(alpha-1/2) * reverse((j+s)^(-alpha)), alpha in [0, 0.5).
- Refine the family parameters, add optional constant endpoint terms, and run a
  local simulated-annealing / block-mutation search.
- Keep the best exact sequence found within the time budget.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2_at_least(m: int) -> int:
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _conv_full(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    n = a.size
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    size = _pow2_at_least(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)[:out_len]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def _conv_valid_large_small(w: np.ndarray, a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    # w length >= a length.
    # Returns valid convolution D[j] = sum_m w[j+m] * a[m], j=0..len(a)-1.
    n = a.size
    total = w.size + n - 1
    size = _pow2_at_least(total)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], size)
    full = np.fft.irfft(fw * fa, size)
    return full[n - 1 : n - 1 + n]


def _score_fast(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Fast exact score matching evaluate_sequence for valid nonnegative inputs."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_full(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _make_base_c(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """a[j] = binom(2j,j)/4^j for j=0..n-1."""
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for j in range(1, n):
        c[j] = c[j - 1] * ((2 * j - 1) / (2 * j))
    return c


def _make_family_a(base_c: np.ndarray, n: int, alpha: float, amp: float, shift: float, x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    alpha = float(alpha)
    amp = float(amp)
    shift = float(shift)
    if not np.isfinite(alpha) or not np.isfinite(amp) or not np.isfinite(shift):
        return base_c.copy()
    if alpha < 0.0:
        alpha = 0.0
    if alpha > 0.4999:
        alpha = 0.4999
    if amp < 0.0:
        amp = 0.0
    if shift <= 1e-6:
        shift = 1e-6
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + shift
    else:
        x = x + shift
    if alpha <= 1e-12:
        tail = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        tail = np.power(x, -alpha)
    scale = amp * (float(n) ** (alpha - 0.5))
    a = base_c + scale * tail[::-1]
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def _family_search(base_c: np.ndarray, n: int, x: np.ndarray, deadline: float):
    best_a = base_c.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(best_a)
    best_params = np.array([0.0, 0.0, 1.0])  # alpha, amp, shift

    alpha_list = np.linspace(0.0, 0.499, 37)
    shift_list = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0)
    amp_list = (0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.18, 0.25, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0)

    for shift in shift_list:
        for alpha in alpha_list:
            for amp in amp_list:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                a = _make_family_a(base_c, n, alpha, amp, shift, x)
                sc = _score_fast(a)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    best_params = np.array([alpha, amp, shift])

    # Coordinate-like refinement of the family parameters.
    lo = np.array([0.0, 0.0, 0.05])
    hi = np.array([0.4999, 3.5, 12.0])
    params = best_params.copy()
    current_score = best_score
    steps = np.array([0.004, 0.04, 0.15])
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    stale = 0

    for outer in range(20000):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        improved = False
        for m in range(3):
            step = steps[m]
            for _ in range(18):
                trial_params = params
                trial_score = current_score
                for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                    tp = params.copy()
                    tp[m] = params[m] + sign * step
                    if tp[m] < lo[m] or tp[m] > hi[m]:
                        continue
                    a = _make_family_a(base_c, n, tp[0], tp[1], tp[2], x)
                    sc = _score_fast(a)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < trial_score:
                        trial_score = sc
                        trial_params = tp
                if trial_score < current_score - 1e-13:
                    params = trial_params
                    current_score = trial_score
                    if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best_score = current_score
                        best_a = _make_family_a(base_c, n, params[0], params[1], params[2], x).copy()
                        best_params = params.copy()
                    improved = True
                    steps[m] *= 1.30
                    stale = 0
                else:
                    steps[m] *= 0.72
                    if steps[m] < (5e-5 if m < 2 else 2e-5):
                        break

        if not improved:
            # Random jitter, accepting only if it improves the global best.
            rp = params.copy()
            rp[0] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.005)
            rp[1] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05)
            rp[2] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.20)
            rp = np.clip(rp, lo, hi)
            a = _make_family_a(base_c, n, rp[0], rp[1], rp[2], x)
            sc = _score_fast(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_params = rp.copy()
                params = rp.copy()
                current_score = sc
                stale = 0
            else:
                stale += 1
                if stale > 600:
                    break

    # Refine adding a tiny constant layer on top of the current best sequence.
    inv_sqrt_n = 1.0 / np.sqrt(float(n))
    const = 0.0
    const_score = best_score
    cstep = 0.05
    cbase = best_a.copy()
    to = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and cstep > 1e-5 and to < 20000:
        to += 1
        found = False
        for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
            c_try = const + sign * cstep
            if c_try < 0.0:
                continue
            a_try = cbase + c_try * inv_sqrt_n
            a_try = np.clip(a_try, 0.0, 1000.0)
            sc = _score_fast(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < const_score - 1e-13:
                const = c_try
                const_score = sc
                cbase = a_try.copy()
                found = True
        if found:
            cstep *= 1.35
        else:
            cstep *= 0.70

    if const_score < best_score - 1e-12:
        best_score = const_score
        best_a = cbase.copy()

    # Refine first/last endpoint multiplicative scales.
    for idx in (0, n - 1):
        fac = 1.0
        fstep = 0.08
        base = best_a.copy()
        base_score = best_score
        to = 0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline and fstep > 1e-5 and to < 20000:
            to += 1
            found = False
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                f_try = fac + sign * fstep
                if f_try <= 0.0 or f_try > 3.0:
                    continue
                a_try = base.copy()
                a_try[idx] = base[idx] * f_try
                a_try = np.clip(a_try, 0.0, 1000.0)
                sc = _score_fast(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < base_score - 1e-13:
                    fac = f_try
                    base_score = sc
                    base = a_try.copy()
                    found = True
            if found:
                fstep *= 1.30
                if base_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = base_score
                    best_a = base.copy()
            else:
                fstep *= 0.72

    return best_a, best_score


def _local_search(a: np.ndarray, deadline: float, seed: int = 99):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    current = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    current_score = _score_fast(current)
    best = current.copy()
    best_score = current_score
    n = current.size

    temp = 0.015
    min_temp = 0.0007
    sigma = 0.18
    stale = 0

    for it in range(2000000):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        r = rng.random()
        if r < 0.42:
            # Block multiplicative perturbation.
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 800), max(2, n // 25)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + width)
            w = stop - start
            cand = current.copy()
            cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
        elif r < 0.78:
            # Sum-preserving pair transfer, biased from early mass to late positions.
            cand = current.copy()
            s = float(np.sum(current))
            if s <= 0.01:
                cand = current
            else:
                p = current / s
                i = int(rng.choice(n, p=p))
                if rng.random() < 0.65:
                    j = int(rng.integers(max(0, n // 4), n))
                else:
                    j = int(rng.choice(n, p=p))
                if i != j:
                    frac = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.60))
                    t = current[i] * frac
                    if t > 0.0 and t < current[i]:
                        cand[i] -= t
                        cand[j] += t
                    else:
                        cand = current
                else:
                    cand = current
        else:
            # Gentle global multiplicative noise.
            cand = current.copy()
            cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * sigma, size=n))

        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1000.0)
        sc = _score_fast(cand)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue

        accept = False
        if sc < current_score:
            accept = True
        elif sc > current_score:
            try:
                q = float(np.exp((current_score - sc) / max(temp, 1e-12)))
            except Exception:
                q = 0.0
            accept = rng.random() < q

        if accept:
            current = cand
            current_score = sc
            if sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = sc
                best = cand.copy()
                stale = 0
                temp = max(min_temp, temp * 0.995)
                sigma = max(0.025, sigma * 0.999)
            else:
                stale += 1
        else:
            temp = max(min_temp, temp * 0.999)
            stale += 1

        if stale > 4000:
            # Reseed from best with a modest perturbation.
            current = best.copy()
            current *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, size=n))
            current = np.clip(current, 0.0, 1000.0)
            current_score = _score_fast(current)
            stale = 0
            temp = max(0.002, min_temp)

        if it % 5000 == 0:
            print("local it", it, "best", best_score)

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed: int = 42, budget_s: float = 1500.0, **kwargs) -> list:
    """Return the best sequence found before the time deadline."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 6.0)

    # Choose a sensible length. The provided parent length is usually ~6962.
    n = 6962
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size >= 512 and np.isfinite(parent).all():
            parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if parent.size > 0 and float(np.sum(parent)) > 0.01:
                n = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Cap extreme lengths to keep FFT searches feasible within the time budget.
    if n < 1024:
        n = 1024
    if n > 16000:
        n = 16000

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    base_c = _make_base_c(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    best_a = base_c.copy()
    best_score = _score_fast(best_a)
    print(f"base len={n}, base score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Phase 1: structured tail-family search.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        best_a, best_score = _family_search(base_c, n, x, deadline)
        print(f"family best score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Phase 2: local search around the best structured sequence.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        local_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, 0.55 * max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))
        best_a, best_score = _local_search(best_a, local_deadline, seed=int(seed * 1000003 + 17))
        print(f"local best score={best_score:.10f}")

    # Final safety: if somehow invalid, fall back to base family.
    if (
        best_a is None
        or not isinstance(best_a, np.ndarray)
        or best_a.size == 0
        or not np.isfinite(best_a).all()
        or not np.isfinite(best_score)
    ):
        best_a = base_c.copy()
        best_score = _score_fast(best_a)

    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    # Ensure sum above evaluator threshold.
    if float(np.sum(out)) < 0.01:
        out = base_c.copy()
    return [float(v) for v in out]
```
